Validity of RED
- Every failure is a Testing Library 'Unable to find…' query or an expect() assertion about missing quota UI or a missing
  quota read. No 'Unexpected API call', TypeError, ReferenceError, import or setup error occurred in this run.
- The suite imports only existing modules (../src/App, Testing Library, Vitest).
- Behaviour-first ordering: Q06, Q07a, Q07b, Q11, Q13 and Q16 perform the user action first, so the failure isolates the
  missing quota refresh. Preserved parts (409 message, no success notice, catalog/requests/loans refresh, cancel and submit
  success) are asserted and passed before the RED assertion.

Investigation notes (pre-commit, not evidence)
- Dry runs on the uncommitted file produced the same 17/4 split.
- The six tests above were reordered behaviour-first, and Q06's assertion was given a path message, before the commit.
- One dry run hit a one-off Vitest collection error ('failed to find the current suite' at tests/setup.ts:4).
  The identical command rerun immediately collected normally. Not counted as evidence.
- Accessible wording asserted here was recorded in #82 before RED (issuecomment-7195791047).

Raw output follows (ANSI colour codes stripped; otherwise verbatim). The stderr section contains Vitest's failure report,
including Testing Library DOM dumps of the synthetic rendered App.
